raw=json.load(open("/tmp/quercetin.json"))
pc=raw["PC_Compounds"][0]
Z={1:"H",6:"C",7:"N",8:"O"}
els=[Z[e] for e in pc["atoms"]["element"]]
heavy=[i for i,e in enumerate(els) if e!="H"]
idx={old:new for new,old in enumerate(heavy)}
qbonds=[(idx[a-1],idx[b-1]) for a,b in zip(pc["bonds"]["aid1"],pc["bonds"]["aid2"]) if a-1 in idx and b-1 in idx]

def readcols(fn):
    out=[]
    for line in open(fn):
        if line.startswith("HETATM"):
            out.append((line[21],line[17:20].strip(),line[76:78].strip() or line[12:14].strip(),
                        float(line[30:38]),float(line[38:46]),float(line[46:54])))
    return out

def readdlg(fn):
    out=[]; inm=False
    for line in open(fn):
        if line.startswith("DOCKED: MODEL"): inm=True
        elif line.startswith("DOCKED: ENDMDL"): break
        elif inm and line.startswith("DOCKED: ATOM"):
            t=line.split(); out.append((t[5],t[4],t[3],float(t[7]),float(t[8]),float(t[9])))
    return out

inp=readcols("hybrid_deca_quercetin.pdb")
d1=readdlg("dlg_4WO5_querc_small.dlg")

P=np.array([a[3:6] for a in inp if a[1]=="DVT"])
Q=np.array([a[3:6] for a in d1 if a[1]=="DVT"])
pm=P.mean(0); qm=Q.mean(0)
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd((P-pm).T@(Q-qm))
R=Vt.T@U.T
if np.linalg.det(R)<0: Vt[-1,:]*=-1; R=Vt.T@U.T
t=qm-R@pm
print("Kabsch RMSD=%.4f"%math.sqrt((((P@R.T+t)-Q)**2).sum()/len(P)))
que=[tuple(R@np.array(a[3:6])+t) for a in inp if a[1]=="QUE"]
elv=[a[2] for a in d1 if a[1]=="DVT"]; dvc=[a[3:6] for a in d1 if a[1]=="DVT"]

with open("/mnt/c/Users/Me/Desktop/CANIFARMA/Resultados/Para enviar/4WO5_deca_quercetin_ATP.pdb","w") as f:
    f.write("REMARK 4WO5 deca-quercetin (Kabsch + CONECT completos)\n")
    s=0
    for el,(x,y,z) in zip(elv,dvc):
        s+=1; nf=(" "+el).ljust(4)
        f.write("HETATM%5d %s DVT A   1    %8.3f%8.3f%8.3f  1.00  0.00          %2s\n"%(s,nf,x,y,z,el))
    for i in range(len(dvc)):
        part=[j+1 for j in range(len(dvc)) if (pqr311) me@TerminalXolotl:/mnt/c/Users/Me/Desktop/CANIFARMA/br_set$
st(dvc[i],dvc[j])<2.1]
        if part: f.write("CONECT%5d%s\n"%(i+1,"".join("%5d"%p for p in sorted(set(part)))))
    off=len(dvc)
    for k,(x,y,z) in enumerate(que):
        s+=1; el=inp[38+k][2]; nf=(" "+el).ljust(4)
        f.write("HETATM%5d %s QUE A   1    %8.3f%8.3f%8.3f  1.00  0.00          %2s\n"%(s,nf,x,y,z,el))
    for i,j in qbonds:
        f.write("CONECT%5d%5d\n"%(off+1+i,off+1+j))
    f.write("END\n")
print("OK 4WO5_deca_quercetin_ATP.pdb reescrito CON CONECT completos")

prot=[]
for line in open("4WO5_rcsb.pdb"):
    if line.startswith("ATOM"):
        prot.append((line[21],line[17:20].strip(),int(line[22:26]),line[12:16].strip(),
                     float(line[30:38]),float(line[38:46]),float(line[46:54])))

CHARGED={"ASP","GLU","ARG","LYS","HIS"}; POLAR={"SER","THR","ASN","GLN","TYR","CYS"}
res={}
for (ch,rn,rs,an,x,y,z) in prot:
    for (lrn,lel,x2,y2,z2) in lig:
        d=math.dist((x,y,z),(x2,y2,z2))
        if d<4.0:
            r=res.setdefault((ch,rn,rs),{"dmin":99,"n":0,"deca":0,"que":0,"hb":0})
            r["n"]+=1; r["dmin"]=min(r["dmin"],d)
            if lrn=="DVT": r["deca"]+=1
            else: r["que"]+=1
            if d<3.5 and lel in "ON" and (an[0] in "NO" or an=="SG"): r["hb"]+=1

print("%-8s %-6s %-7s %-4s %-12s %-6s %s"%("Residuo","dmin","n","Hb","Contacto","Tipo","Rol"))
print("-"*75)
for (ch,rn,rs),r in sorted(res.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1]["dmin"]):
    tipo="CARGADO" if rn in CHARGED else ("polar" if rn in POLAR else "hidrofob")
    parte="deca+que" if r["deca"] and r["que"] else ("deca" if r["deca"] else "que")
    rol=""
    if 530<=rs<=535: rol="HINGE"
    if rn in CHARGED and r["deca"] and r["dmin"]<3.5: rol="puente salino"
    if rn=="TRP" and r["que"]: rol="pi-stacking" if r["dmin"]<4 else rol
    print("%s%s%-3d  %.2f   %-6d %-4d %-12s %-7s %s"%(rn,ch,rs,r["dmin"],r["n"],r["hb"],parte,tipo,rol))

print("\n=== INTERACCIONES CLAVE (<3.5 A) ===")

    res={}
    for (pch,rn,rs,an,x,y,z) in prot:

## APÉNDICE A: MAPA DE INTERACCIONES DEL BOMBARDEO DUAL (4WO5)

### Complejo #1 (ΔG = −10.35 kcal/mol) — puente inter-cadenas
| Residuo | Cadena | dmin (Å) | H-bonds | Contacto | Rol |
|---------|--------|----------|---------|----------|-----|
| GLU586  | A | 2.64 | 2 | quercetin | H-bond |
| TRP531  | A | 2.69 | 1 | quercetin | π-stacking |
| GLU533  | A | 2.74 | 2 | quercetin | hinge, H-bond |
| **ARG509** | **B** | 2.82 | 1 | deca+que | **puente salino (hotspot del dímero)** |
| HIS585  | A | 3.06 | 2 | quercetin | H-bond |
| ASP587  | A | 3.48 | 0 | quercetin | contacto |
| HIS510  | B | 3.49 | 1 | deca | puente salino |
| CYS532  | A | 3.55 | 0 | quercetin | hinge |

### Complejo #2 (ΔG = −7.51 kcal/mol) — parche hidrofóbico αC/β3
| Residuo | Cadena | dmin (Å) | H-bonds | Contacto | Rol |
|---------|--------|----------|---------|----------|-----|
| TRP450  | B | 2.64 | 4 | deca+que | π-stacking + H-bonds |
| PHE516  | B | 2.78 | 1 | quercetin | hidrofóbico |
| MET517  | B | 2.80 | 0 | quercetin | hidrofóbico |
| LEU515  | B | 3.46 | 0 | quercetin | hidrofóbico |
| HIS477  | B | 3.68 | 0 | quercetin | contacto |

### Conclusión del mapa
- **Cero residuos compartidos** entre C1 y C2 → sitios independientes → aditividad termodinámica válida (ΔG_int C1–C2 = +0.09 kcal/mol ≈ 0).
- C1 = modo electrostático/H-bond cruzando ambas cadenas (bloquea el hotspot Arg509).
- C2 = modo hidrofóbico en el parche αC/β3 de la cadena B.
- La combinación de ambos modos cubre ~30 Å de la interfaz del dímero (avididad por ocupación multivalente).
